- **token-burning-mode**: 指定されたAI利用枠を承認済みの価値ある仕事へ集中配分し、品質とHumanの権限を保って、利用可能な時間内に残量を限りなくゼロへ近づける。トークン消費モードの適用や、残量を集中的に成果へ変える依頼に使う。残量確認・Reset券の報告だけでは消費へ進まない。相談・Plan-only・引用・本Skillの設計改訂を実行依頼へ読み替えず、Resetは別途委任された対象と条件に限る。 ([yusukefujiijp/ai-project](https://github.com/yusukefujiijp/ai-project/tree/HEAD/skills/token-burning-mode))
- **token-calculation**: Use when auditing the context-size, token-budget, or latency of ANY repository from its docs/ folder and its Claude Code session transcripts — questions like "how big is our docs corpus", "why are sessions on this repo getting slow or expensive", corpus growth, giant-doc tail, onboarding read-amplification, or context-vs-latency. Read-only; produces a measured report. For an agent-playbook role/skill static-load audit, pair with token-playbook-calculation. ([hameddk/hsdk-skills](https://github.com/hameddk/hsdk-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/token-calculation))
- **token-calculator**: Calculates token counts, character lengths, and context window utilization for text strings or local codebase files. ([shortprompt-blip/awesome-ai-tools-and-utilities](https://github.com/shortprompt-blip/awesome-ai-tools-and-utilities/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/token-calculator))
- **token-cap**: Use when working on the Route harness and you need cost efficiency control: Enforces token ceilings per route so a single dispatch cannot overspend. ([AesopScott/mojo](https://github.com/AesopScott/mojo/tree/HEAD/harnesses/skills/token-cap))
- **token-capped-workflow**: Design a task workflow under an explicit token cap (e.g. 10k total) — allocate budget per phase, verify the most critical part first, and degrade gracefully when the cap nears. Use when asked to 'giới hạn token', 'làm trong 10k token', 'token cap cho task này', 'budget workflow', 'tiết kiệm chi phí cho task', 'cheap mode', or 'work within a token limit'. Do NOT use for: choosing response length/depth — see token-budget-advisor. Do NOT use for: auditing context window bloat — see context-budget. Do NOT use for: cost-per-value scoring — see token-roi. ([Horonomos-laps/Yana-AI](https://github.com/Horonomos-laps/Yana-AI/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/token-capped-workflow) · ★13 · 4 copies)
- **token-capture**: Capture and print the bearer token used by Claude API calls. Use when the user wants to automate extracting the Authorization token from requests to /v1/messages. ([vargos-labs/vargos](https://github.com/vargos-labs/vargos/tree/HEAD/.templates/agent/skills/token-capture) · ★2)
